# corbel vs grep / ripgrep / ripgrep+ctags benchmark

`ripgrep+ctags` is a hybrid, not plain ctags: call-site discovery comes from ripgrep (or grep if ripgrep is unavailable), and ctags supplies only the enclosing-scope/end-line lookup for those hits (definition lookup is the one task answered by ctags alone). Plain ctags has no call-site index and could not attempt the callers task at all, which is why this harness measures the hybrid a real user would reach for instead of a strawman zero score.

**The callees (T2) task was skipped in this run (callees (T2) excluded by default: ~90% of golden-set callees ground truth is empty (2/55 chevrotain, 2/33 hyperfine, 4/21 itsdangerous non-empty), so scoring it grades tools against 'no answer' rather than against real ground truth. Pass --include-callees to run it anyway.). No callees rows appear in the tables below. Pass `--include-callees` to include it.**

Run started: 2026-09-03T09:24:03.192771+00:00
Run finished: 2026-09-03T09:24:06.453325+00:00

## Tool versions

| Tool | Version |
| --- | --- |
| corbel | corbel 0.1.0 |
| grep | grep (GNU grep) 3.12 |
| ripgrep | ripgrep 15.2.0 |
| ctags | Universal Ctags 6.2.1, Copyright (C) 2015-2025 Universal Ctags Team |
| python | 3.14.7 |
| os | Linux 7.1.9-200.fc44.x86_64 |

Accuracy runs use `BENCHMARK_TOKEN_BUDGET = 1000000` for every corbel `get_symbol` call, not corbel's own built-in default, so truncation cannot silently depress recall.

Rationale: corbel's get_symbol splits its token_budget first across every matched symbol row, then splits each row's share evenly between callers and callees, so any nonzero-but-realistic budget can silently drop entries for a symbol with a large caller or callee list before precision/recall is ever computed. Accuracy runs must never be affected by this: 1,000,000 is three orders of magnitude above the largest caller count in this benchmark's golden sets (itsdangerous-01 want_bytes, 19 callers, each estimated at roughly 20-30 tokens by corbel's own estimate_node_tokens), while still being a finite, explicit value rather than an unbounded one.

## Repository: chevrotain (typescript)

- Expected commit: `221fff76526021a3abba1068130f5e8a343fbaaf`
- Actual commit at run time: `221fff76526021a3abba1068130f5e8a343fbaaf`
- Commit match: yes
- Search scope: `packages/chevrotain/src` (all four tools, including corbel's repo-wide index results, are restricted to this path before scoring)
- Search scope rationale: Monorepo. Candidate symbols are restricted to packages/chevrotain/src per task scope; candidate_scanner.py was run with --scope-subdir packages/chevrotain/src against the full repo checkout so 'file' values stay relative to the monorepo root (matching lsp_to_draft.py's and typescript-language-server's expected paths), not to the subdir. A small number of verification_notes cite files outside this scope (packages/gast/src/visitor.ts, packages/types/api.d.ts) purely as read-only context to establish why an in-scope symbol's calls are ambiguous or absent - no symbol from outside packages/chevrotain/src was ever selected as a candidate or scored entry.
- corbel index time: 0.073s
- ctags build time: 0.149s

### Truncated cases

None. Every corbel call in this repository fit within the 1000000-token accuracy budget; no precision/recall number below was affected by truncation.
